### KAVACH
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/kavach-f855) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/rahulsharma767/infinityHack.git) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://infinity-hack-git-main-rahul810840-5774s-projects.vercel.app/) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://youtu.be/L-eRB-yD1-I) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-Infinity%20Hacks%202026-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://infinity-hacks.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-17-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> DETECT •PREDICT• PREVENT

![React](https://img.shields.io/badge/React-333333?style=flat-square) ![JavaScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/JavaScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Machine Learning](https://img.shields.io/badge/Machine%20Learning-333333?style=flat-square) ![Deep Learning](https://img.shields.io/badge/Deep%20Learning-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![XGBoost](https://img.shields.io/badge/XGBoost-333333?style=flat-square) ![JSX](https://img.shields.io/badge/JSX-333333?style=flat-square) ![YOLOv3 Algorithm](https://img.shields.io/badge/YOLOv3%20Algorithm-333333?style=flat-square)

**Challenges we ran into**

One of the key challenges was training YOLO for wildlife detection. Finding suitable datasets for Indian wildlife species was difficult, as many publicly available datasets did not sufficiently match the species relevant to our use case. Preparing suitable training data and getting reliable detection performance required significant experimentation.

We also faced challenges in sourcing location-based datasets that could support meaningful risk intelligence for wildlife conflict-prone areas.

Another major hurdle was integrating the complete technology stack. Connecting the trained YOLO model with our ML-based risk intelligence, backend, and frontend required careful handling of data flow and APIs so that detection results could be processed and displayed correctly.

We overcame these challenges through iterative dataset preparation, model training and testing, and step-by-step integration of the YOLO, ML, backend, and frontend components into a single working system

**The problem it solves**

Human-wildlife conflict poses a significant safety challenge for communities located near forest and wildlife habitats. Existing monitoring and response mechanisms can be difficult to manage across large areas, often resulting in delayed awareness and response when wildlife enters human-populated zones.

KAVACH addresses this challenge by providing a centralized platform for monitoring wildlife activity, identifying potential conflict-prone areas, and supporting timely decision-making.

The system can be used to:

Monitor wildlife activity across vulnerable areas.
Identify locations with a higher likelihood of human-wildlife encounters.
Provide timely alerts to relevant authorities and stakeholders.
Visualize wildlife incidents and risk-prone zones on a geographical interface.
Maintain historical information to identify recurring conflict patterns.
Support forest officials in planning preventive measures and allocating resources more effectively.

By bringing monitoring, location-based information, alerts, and historical insights into a single platform, KAVACH aims to make wildlife conflict management more proactive, organized, and effective.

**Wildlife Protection**

KAVACH directly addresses human-wildlife conflict by providing a technology-driven platform for wildlife monitoring and risk intelligence. The system uses YOLO-based wildlife detection to identify animals from images/video and combines detection results with location-based risk intelligence to identify and understand conflict-prone areas. By integrating wildlife detection, ML-based risk analysis, backend services, and a user-facing dashboard, KAVACH supports earlier awareness and more informed decision-making for wildlife protection and human safety.

Team **TwoPointers** -- [Tejaswee Rajput](https://github.com/tejaswee-06), [Sohana Pilli](https://github.com/Sohana881), [SHREYA DUBEY](https://github.com/shreya-20-2003), [Rahul Sharma](https://github.com/rahulsharma767)

**The problem it solves**

## The problem it solves

This project transforms manual railway track fitting management into a digital, AI-assisted workflow. It allows operators to:

- generate and attach laser-marked QR codes to track fittings such as rail clips, pads, liners, and sleepers
- scan QR codes using mobile devices for instant part identification
- track inventory, vendor details, and installation history centrally
- automate inspection reports and monitor quality with AI analytics
- improve safety by reducing manual record-keeping and minimizing human error

In short, the system makes track maintenance faster, more reliable, and easier to audit.

- **Integration:** Multi-service setup was complex  
- **QR scanning:** Device compatibility issues  
- **Deployment:** Env setup & scripts needed consistency

### PayShield
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/payshield-8449) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/deveshreddyp/paysheild) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://payshield-dashboard.onrender.com/) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://youtu.be/99fMDsyHzA4) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-Hack--Nocturne%202.O-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://hack-nocturne-2.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-14-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> Real-time ML defense against API tampering

![React](https://img.shields.io/badge/React-333333?style=flat-square) ![Node.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![JavaScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/JavaScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![Flask](https://img.shields.io/badge/Flask-333333?style=flat-square) ![Machine Learning](https://img.shields.io/badge/Machine%20Learning-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![TypeScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/TypeScript-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

Financial technology is engaged in a continuous arms race. While traditional security focuses on authenticating who is making a request, sophisticated attackers exploit what is in the request. Modern platforms are highly vulnerable to Man-in-the-Middle (MitM) API Tampering and complex, multi-node Fraud Rings.

Attackers routinely bypass standard authentication to intercept and modify in-flight API payloads—for example, altering a checkout price from $500.00 to $5.00, manipulating payout routing numbers, or inflating refund quantities. Traditional rule-based Web Application Firewalls (WAFs) fail because the requests themselves are structurally valid and properly authenticated; they simply lack the contextual intelligence to recognize that the intent of the data has been maliciously altered.

PayShield is a real-time, ML-powered defense layer that solves this critical vulnerability.

Deep Payload Inspection: It doesn't just check tokens; it actively analyzes the integrity of incoming API parameters against historical user baselines.
Intelligent Network Defense: By leveraging GraphSAGE (Graph Neural Networks), PayShield maps complex transaction networks to identify and dismantle coordinated, multi-account fraud rings before an exploited transaction can settle.
Zero-Trust Transactions: It provides a lightweight middleware gateway paired with an intuitive Next.js dashboard, transforming naive APIs into zero-trust, tamper-proof endpoints.

**Challenges we ran into**

Building a low-latency security gateway that relies on heavy machine learning inference presented several significant technical hurdles:

⚡ Latency vs. Security (The Real-Time Dilemma): We initially implemented GraphSAGE inferences inline with our API traffic. This introduced unacceptable latency overhead (500ms+) for standard transactions, which ruins the user experience in modern fin-tech.
The Fix: We completely re-architected our pipeline. We implemented a split-architecture approach. We built a lightning-fast, highly optimized anomaly detection heuristic (using Random Forests and FAISS similarity matching) at the synchronous gateway level to instantly block clear tampering attempts. We then pushed the computationally intensive GraphSAGE node-embeddings inference to an asynchronous background worker that flags suspicious graph interactions (like high-velocity cyclical transfers) without blocking the critical path.
⚔️ Constructing a "White-Hat" Attack Vector: To train our models and empirically prove that our gateway intercepts tampered requests, we had to become the attacker. We needed a reliable way to execute MitM attacks on our own localhost environments without building a sprawling, custom malicious proxy.
The Fix: We integrated Requestly into our development loop. This allowed us to quickly author exact HTTP modification rules and reliably intercept, dissect, and poison our own frontend requests before they hit the backend, providing robust validation for our Tamper Alerts.
🔄 Stateful WebSockets in Next.js: Ensuring our TamperAlert.tsx dashboard accurately reflected real-time blocking events from the Node.js middleware required strict state management. We had to implement robust WebSocket reconnect logic to prevent dropped packets during localized server restarts.

**Creative Use of Requestly**

Track: Creative Use of Requestly - The "White-Hat Attack Simulator"

To demonstrate the power of a security product, you have to prove it stops an attack. We creatively deployed Requestly not just as a developer utility, but as the core Attack Simulation Engine for our entire project demonstration.

PayShield is designed to catch invisible, in-flight API tampering. To showcase this during the hackathon, we utilized Requestly's Modify Request Body rules to simulate a targeted Man-in-the-Middle exploit.

The Workflow:

A user attempts a standard checkout (e.g., Transfer $100.00) via our React frontend.
The Requestly Extension intercepts this legitimate XHR/Fetch request in transit.
According to our custom rule, Requestly maliciously alters the payload structure (e.g., changing the amount to $1.00 or swapping the destination_account_id).
The poisoned request hits the PayShield Backend API.
Our ML models instantly recognize the structural anomaly, block the transaction, and fire a WebSocket alert to our Live Tamper Dashboard.
Requestly was absolutely critical to our success. It allowed us to flawlessly, repeatedly, and visibly trigger PayShield's core machine learning defenses on-demand, transforming an invisible backend process into a highly visual, winning demonstration.

Team **ORBIT** -- [Devesh Reddy](https://github.com/deveshreddyp), [Nihal DR](https://github.com/NihalDR), [tanusree reddy](https://github.com/m-tanusree-reddy)

### RoadSense AI
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/roadsense-ai-2c65) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/Ayan786151/RoadSense-AI) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/10R-Or3w2noonGZ1Yi3qaMZgCKGMP5o6G?usp=sharing) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-DOMINION%202026-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://dominion2026.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-11-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> AI based Risk Prediction Traffic Control System

![HTML](https://img.shields.io/badge/HTML-333333?style=flat-square) ![CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/CSS-333333?style=flat-square) ![JavaScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/JavaScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![OpenCV](https://img.shields.io/badge/OpenCV-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![JSON](https://img.shields.io/badge/JSON-333333?style=flat-square) ![Streamlit](https://img.shields.io/badge/Streamlit-333333?style=flat-square) ![Reinforcement learning](https://img.shields.io/badge/Reinforcement%20learning-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

**The Problem: The Flaw of Reactive Governance**

Road and transport safety is one of the most critical civic challenges in India, claiming over 1.5 lakh lives every year. Traditional municipal traffic management is fundamentally crippled by a reactive loop. By the time the issue is resolved, the damage is already done, lives are lost, and hours of citizen productivity are wasted. Traffic lights operate on dumb, static countdown timers oblivious to real vehicle queues, and police deployment happens after a hotspot has already turned fatal.


**The Solution: Answering the Proactive Question**

RoadSense AI flips this paradigm entirely. Instead of waiting for disaster to strike, our system acts on the proactive side. Based on live vehicle kinematics, temporal patterns, and environmental factors, it determines and predicts where the next critical accident hotspot or bottleneck will emerge, and how do we dynamically intervene right now to prevent it from ever happening?

**RoadSense AI answers this through an end-to-end intelligence ecosystem:**

**Real-Time Vision & Kinematics**: Using YOLOv11 detection and ByteTrack multi-object tracking on live CCTV streams, the system monitors  traffic flow, calculating true ground-plane speeds and spatial density via calibrated 4-point perspective homography.

**Dynamic Adaptive Signal Control (DTSC)**: Rather than relying on rigid fixed-timer green lights, our dynamic algorithm calculates real-time green lights to track and monitor traffic violations, serving as a data parameter.

**Longitudinal ML Risk Forecasting**: A leakage-free Supervised ML Risk Forecaster (Random Forest with strict chronological temporal validation) analyzes 50 municipal zones over 52 weeks to forecast future accident probabilities, alerting authorities to deploy preventative patrols and speed-calming measures days before accidents occur.

**Autonomous AI Safety Briefings**: An integrated LLM briefing engine (Llama-3.3-70B / Gemini-2.5-Flash) translates complex kinematic matrices and predictive probabilities into plain-English action-ready guidelines and briefings.

**Flexible Architecture (The highlight of our project)**: 
To bridge the gap between municipal authorities and everyday commuters, RoadSense AI features a flexible public API promoting code visibility. We empower external developers, urban researchers, and civic hackers to build commuter alert apps, navigation aids, and third-party civic tools without reinventing the underlying vision or predictive infrastructure. To show an example we built a civilian frontend as well. 

**Local Admin Command Center (Streamlit)**: An analytical powerhouse built for traffic controllers and municipal planners. It delivers deep telemetry, live CCTV calibration controls, DTSC signal overrides, machine learning diagnostics, and predictive zone ranking leaderboards.

**Civilian Transparency Portal (Web Frontend)**: A lightweight, fast, accessible interface built for the general public. It provides citizens with transparent, real-time visibility into crossroad telemetry, current signal phases, queue states, and road safety indices without overwhelming administrative complexity.

**Challenges we ran into**

**Scarcity of Public Indian Road Datasets**: Unlike Western cities with granular open-data portals, structured zone-by-zone Indian traffic accident and speed telemetry is virtually non-existent in the public domain. We had to invest immense effort researching municipal patterns to establish baseline benchmarks.

**Simulating Databases for Chaotic Indian Road Realities**: Indian roads cannot be modeled using generic simulators designed for lane-disciplined Western traffic. We had to build a custom simulation pipeline (sim.py) that models high-density two-wheeler swarms, auto-rickshaws, erratic lane adherence, festival surges, and monsoon weather severity across 50 municipal sectors.

**Calibrating Predictive Factor Weightage**: Balancing feature weights was a major machine learning challenge. Determining how much influence variables like vehicle density, average speed delta, heavy-vehicle ratio, road surface condition, and weather severity should hold required extensive correlation analyses, SHAP evaluations, and hyperparameter tuning to avoid bias.

**Perspective Homography Calibration**: Converting 2D CCTV pixel coordinates into accurate real-world metric speeds required mapping arbitrary camera angles to a bird's-eye ground plane via 4-point quad homography transforms across varying roadway geometries.

**Eliminating Temporal Data Leakage**: Standard random cross-validation catastrophically leaks future trends into past training on time-series panel data. We engineered a strict chronological split along with rolling and lagged temporal feature extractors to ensure the risk forecaster remains strictly causal and predictive.

Team **dawg** -- Sayak Palit, Ayan Hussain, Antarip Nandi, SOUMYA SAHARAY

### Pounce
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/pounce-ab42) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/AmrendratheCoder/teampounce) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://teampounce.vercel.app/) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://youtu.be/7MG6wZK1qDM) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-Agentic%20Commerce%20Hackathon-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://agentic-commerce.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-7-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> Shopping you walk into, together.

![Node.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![Deep Learning](https://img.shields.io/badge/Deep%20Learning-333333?style=flat-square) ![WebSockets](https://img.shields.io/badge/WebSockets-333333?style=flat-square) ![Three.JS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Three.JS-333333?style=flat-square) ![TypeScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/TypeScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![OpenAi](https://img.shields.io/badge/OpenAi-333333?style=flat-square) ![Tailwind CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tailwind%20CSS-333333?style=flat-square)

**Challenges we ran into**

# Technical Challenges

Building Pounce was far more than creating another e-commerce website. It required solving engineering challenges across **real-time graphics, networking, AI, machine learning, and distributed systems**. Every feature from a living 3D shopping world to AI employees and secure background automation had to work together seamlessly while remaining responsive and scalable for thousands of concurrent users.

- **Building a Living 3D World**  
  Creating a realistic shopping experience using **Three.js** was one of the biggest challenges. We had to build an optimized 3D environment with interactive stores, realistic lighting, animated products, and NPCs that continuously walk around, browse items, and perform natural actions so the world feels alive instead of static.

- **Character Animation & Movement**  
  Designing player avatars with smooth walking, running, idle, and interaction animations required careful animation blending and synchronization. Every movement had to feel natural while remaining responsive across different devices.

- **Real-Time Multiplayer & Voice Communication**  
  Synchronizing multiple players inside the same virtual world with minimal latency was a major challenge. Player positions, animations, interactions, and **real-time speech-to-speech communication** all needed to stay perfectly synchronized to create a natural collaborative shopping experience.

- **Multi-Agent AI System**  
  Instead of relying on a single chatbot, we built a **multi-agent architecture** where each AI employee specializes in a specific tasks - greeter, sales associate, stockroom assistant, and cashier. Managing agent coordination, conversation context, and smooth handoffs between them required careful orchestration.

- **Real-Time AR Try-On**  
  Unlike traditional virtual try-on systems that rely on uploading an image, our goal was a **live AR experience**. This required machine learning models capable of real-time body and foot tracking, accurate product alignment, and low-latency rendering while users move naturally.

- **Scalable Background Workers**  
  Features like **Pounce Watcher** continuously monitor product availability and pricing. Designing a background worker architecture capable of processing thousands of monitoring jobs simultaneously without impacting user-facing services was a significant engineering challenge.

- **Redis, Queues & Distributed Processing**  
  Managing large-scale asynchronous workloads required careful use of **Redis**, distributed queues, caching, job retries, duplicate prevention, worker recovery, and queue prioritization to keep the system reliable under heavy traffic.

- **Performance & Scalability**  
  Bringing together real-time 3D rendering, multiplayer networking, AI agents, AR processing, voice communication, and background automation while maintaining a smooth experience for thousands of concurrent users required extensive optimization across the entire stack.

**The problem it solves**

# The Problem

Online shopping today is a **solitary experience**. You scroll through endless product images with no shopkeeper to consult, no friend beside you, and little confidence that a product will meet your expectations. Most decisions rely on **anonymous reviews and ratings**, which can be misleading or manipulated.

Meanwhile, AI shopping agents expect users to trust them with purchases and payment credentials, hoping they choose the right product without overspending. That isn't true trust.

## What is Pounce?

**Pounce** transforms online shopping into a **shared 3D retail experience**. Enter a virtual store with friends, interact with AI-powered staff, explore products from live merchant catalogs, try products in AR, and pay securely through **Prava**, ensuring every purchase stays within the limits you approve.

## Key Features

- **Shop Together** — Walk through the same virtual store with friends, discuss products in real time, and make purchase decisions collaboratively instead of shopping alone.

- **Real Reviews from Real Owners** — Talk directly to people who already own a product and hear genuine first-hand experiences instead of relying only on anonymous ratings.

- **AI Store Staff** — Meet specialized AI assistants including a greeter, sales associate, stockroom assistant, and cashier, creating the experience of a real retail store.

- **AR Try-On** — See products such as shoes or accessories on yourself in real time before making a purchase.

- **Secure Payments with Prava** — Approve purchases with a passkey while your card details remain hidden. Prava issues a **single-use credential** limited to one merchant and one approved amount, preventing unauthorized spending.

- **Pounce Watcher** — Set conditions like *"Buy this when my size is back in stock,"* and Pounce automatically monitors availability and completes the purchase when your condition is met.

## Why Pounce?

Pounce makes shopping **social, trustworthy, and secure**. Instead of replacing human decisions, it combines real conversations, immersive shopping, AI assistance, and secure payments  by keeping users fully in control while making online shopping feel like visiting a real store.

**Best Visa Intelligent Commerce Implementation**

Pounce's checkout is powered by **Visa Intelligent Commerce through Prava**, enabling AI agents to complete purchases without ever exposing a user's payment credentials. At checkout, users approve the purchase with a **passkey**, after which Prava issues a **single-use Visa payment credential** that is strictly **scoped to one merchant and one approved amount**. The AI agent completes the transaction using this credential, while the user's actual card details never reach either Pounce or the merchant.

The same security model extends to **autonomous purchasing** through **Pounce Watcher**. During setup, users authorize a **Visa-backed Prava mandate** with a one-time passkey, defining the maximum amount and the specific merchant the agent is allowed to purchase from. When the user's condition such as a price drop or product restock is met, the watcher securely executes the purchase within those predefined limits. Prava again generates a **single-use Visa credential**, allowing the AI agent to complete the checkout automatically without requiring another approval.

## Why It Matters

Traditional AI shopping agents require users to trust the model with their payment information. Pounce removes that trust assumption by building on **Prava's implementation of Visa Intelligent Commerce**, where security is enforced by the payment infrastructure itself rather than the AI agent.

Through **Prava**, users benefit from:

- **Card details are never shared** with either Pounce or the merchant.
- **Single-use Visa payment credentials** that expire immediately after a successful transaction.
- **Merchant- and amount-scoped credentials**, ensuring payments can only be used for the approved merchant and spending limit.
- **Visa-backed Prava mandates** for autonomous purchases, allowing payments only within the limits the user has pre-approved.
- **Passkey-based authorization**, ensuring users remain in control while AI agents securely complete the shopping workflow.

By combining Pounce's AI-powered shopping experience with **Prava's secure payment infrastructure built on Visa Intelligent Commerce**, agentic commerce becomes both autonomous and trustworthy.

**Most Startup-Ready Product**

Pounce is built to be a **real product**, not just a hackathon demo. It is the **social, trustworthy front door for agentic commerce**, combining immersive shopping, AI agents, AR, multiplayer experiences, and secure payments powered by **Prava**.

## Why Pounce is Startup Ready

* **Production Ready Product**  
  Pounce already delivers a complete end to end shopping experience. Users can enter a 3D virtual store, interact with specialized AI staff, browse live merchant catalogs, try products on using AR, and complete secure purchases through Prava with passkey authentication. Features like **Pounce Watcher** automatically purchase products when user defined conditions such as price drops or restocks are met.

* **Clear Customer Segments**  
  Pounce serves two distinct audiences:
  * **Shoppers** looking for a social, immersive, and trustworthy alternative to traditional online shopping.
  * **Merchants** who want an AI powered 3D storefront connected to their existing catalog while using Prava's secure payment infrastructure without handling sensitive card information.

## Revenue Model

Pounce combines marketplace revenue with a token economy that continuously encourages purchases and user engagement.

* **Commission on Every Order**  
  Pounce earns a commission on every successful purchase completed through the platform using Prava.

* **Purchase Rewards Through Tokens**  
  Every successful purchase rewards users with **Pounce Tokens**. Since tokens are earned only through real purchases, the ecosystem naturally encourages more shopping, generating more merchant sales and increasing platform revenue.

* **Avatar Skins and Cosmetics**  
  Users can spend earned tokens on avatar customization, while premium cosmetic items are available for direct purchase, creating a high margin digital revenue stream.

* **Token Powered Discounts**  
  Users can redeem accumulated tokens for discount coupons on future purchases, encouraging repeat shopping and improving long term retention.

* **Merchant Platform Fees**  
  Brands can launch customized 3D storefronts inside Pounce and pay recurring subscription or setup fees for branded experiences, in addition to marketplace commissions.

## Why Users Keep Coming Back

* **Real Reviews from Real Owners**  
  Instead of relying on anonymous ratings, users can speak directly with people who already own a product inside the multiplayer world and receive genuine first hand feedback before making a purchase.

* **A Social Shopping Destination**  
  Pounce is more than a checkout page. Users can meet friends, discover new people, explore stores together, and spend time inside a living virtual world.

* **Rewards on Every Purchase**  
  Tokens, cosmetic unlocks, and discount coupons create continuous incentives for users to shop through Pounce instead of directly from a merchant's website.

* **A Platform That Continues to Grow**  
  Pounce can naturally expand with future experiences such as full VR support, interactive games, live events, and community spaces, transforming it into both a shopping platform and an entertainment destination.

## Virality and Network Effects

Pounce becomes more valuable as its community grows.

* More shoppers create a richer multiplayer experience.
* More verified product owners provide more trustworthy recommendations.
* Friends naturally invite friends to shop together.
* The token economy rewards engagement and encourages repeat participation.

Every new user makes the platform more social, more informative, and more engaging, creating strong organic network effects.

## One Line Pitch

**Shopping evolved from physical stores into lonely product pages. Pounce brings the store back with AI powered staff, multiplayer shopping, live AR try ons, authentic human recommendations, secure Prava payments, and a token economy that rewards every purchase, building the consumer front door for the agentic commerce era.**

**OpenAI**

OpenAI serves as the **reasoning engine** behind Pounce's multi-agent shopping experience. Rather than using a single chatbot, Pounce is built around **five specialized AI agents** that work together to recreate the experience of shopping inside a real retail store.

## How OpenAI Powers Pounce

- **Specialized AI Employees**  
  Every AI employee in Pounce has a dedicated responsibility. The **Greeter** welcomes users and understands their intent, the **Sales Associate** recommends products based on preferences, the **Stockroom Helper** checks availability and reserves inventory, the **Fitting Room Assistant** guides users through the AR try-on experience, and the **Cashier** prepares the final checkout. Each agent focuses on its own task while collaborating with the others to deliver a seamless shopping journey.

- **Natural Shopping Conversations**  
  Users interact with AI employees through natural voice or text conversations instead of navigating traditional filters. The agents understand shopping intent, answer product-related questions, compare alternatives, explain recommendations, and guide users through the store just like a knowledgeable salesperson.

- **Intelligent Agent Collaboration**  
  The shopping experience is powered by multiple agents working together rather than independently. As a user progresses through the store, conversations and context flow naturally between agents, creating a continuous experience from product discovery to checkout without requiring the user to repeat information.

- **Personalized Decision Making**  
  Every agent has access to the user's shopping persona and preferences through Senso. Recommendations are based on the user's style, budget, favorite brands, previous purchases, and shopping history instead of generic product rankings.

- **Action-Oriented AI**  
  The agents do more than answer questions. They actively search live merchant catalogs, verify product information, check inventory, reserve products, prepare checkout, and coordinate with Prava to complete secure purchases, making them active participants in the shopping journey rather than passive assistants.

## Why OpenAI Makes Pounce Different

Most AI shopping assistants function as conversational chatbots that simply answer questions. Pounce uses OpenAI to build a **true multi-agent retail ecosystem**, where AI employees collaborate just like staff inside a physical store.

- **Five specialized AI employees** instead of one generic assistant.
- **Natural conversations** that replace traditional product search and filtering.
- **Collaborative agent workflows** that mirror the experience of visiting a real store.
- **Personalized recommendations** powered by persistent user memory from Senso.
- **Real-world actions**, including product discovery, inventory management, checkout preparation, and secure purchasing through Prava.

OpenAI is the intelligence layer that enables every AI employee to reason, collaborate, and assist users throughout the complete shopping journey, transforming online shopping into an immersive, human-like retail experience.

**Agent Commerce Discovery & Trust**

Senso is the **context, memory, and knowledge layer** behind Pounce. It enables every AI agent to make decisions using verified information and persistent user context rather than isolated prompts. From powering the Drops feed to delivering personalized recommendations, Senso ensures every shopping experience becomes smarter over time.

## How Senso Powers Pounce

- **Verified Drops Feed**  
  Every new product drop, including its name, merchant, price, availability, and metadata, is ingested into Senso's knowledge base. When users browse the **Drops** tab, Pounce queries Senso to retrieve and verify each product, allowing every drop to display a **"Verified via Senso"** badge backed by a trusted source of truth instead of an unverified product listing.

- **Personalized From Day One**  
  During onboarding, users define their shopping preferences such as **style, budget, favorite colors, brands, and priorities**. This shopper persona is stored in Senso, allowing every AI agent to understand the user's preferences from the very first interaction and recommend products that genuinely match their taste.

- **Continuous Learning**  
  Every purchase, product interaction, and shopping decision is written back to Senso. This persistent memory enables the AI agents to continuously refine their understanding of the user, making future recommendations increasingly accurate and personalized.

- **Shared Context Across All AI Agents**  
  Every specialized AI employee, from the Sales Associate to the Cashier, accesses the same contextual memory stored in Senso. This allows conversations to remain consistent throughout the shopping journey without forcing users to repeat their preferences or previous decisions.

## Why Senso Makes Pounce Different

Most shopping platforms rely on **generic recommendation engines** that have little understanding of individual users and often surface products without meaningful verification.

With Senso, Pounce provides:

- **Verified product drops** backed by a structured knowledge base.
- **Persistent user memory** that carries across every shopping session.
- **Personalized recommendations** based on long-term preferences instead of one-time prompts.
- **Continuously improving AI agents** that become smarter after every interaction and purchase.
- **Graceful fallback support**, ensuring the experience remains responsive even if cloud-based knowledge services are temporarily unavailable.

By combining **verified knowledge**, **persistent memory**, and **continuous learning**, Senso enables Pounce to deliver a shopping experience that is significantly more **personal, trustworthy, and context-aware** than traditional e-commerce platforms.

![image](https://assets.devfolio.co/content/1d9a73e1b7a84cd0bbe94ea0a01b274f/8ee21a33-f08b-4346-875d-8a97efbdef96.png)

**Best Agentic User Experience**

Pounce is designed to make AI agents feel like **real store employees**, not just another chatbot. Instead of interacting with a single assistant, users meet **five specialized agents** - a **Greeter**, **Sales Associate**, **Stockroom Helper**, **Fitting Room Assistant**, and **Cashier** , each responsible for a specific part of the shopping journey inside an immersive 3D world.

The agents handle the repetitive work, from discovering products and checking inventory to reserving stock and preparing checkout, while the user always remains in control by approving payments with a **passkey**. Beyond AI assistance, users can **try products on in real time using AR** and **talk to real product owners** inside the multiplayer world to hear authentic first-hand experiences.

By combining specialized AI agents, immersive 3D interactions, AR visualization, and real human conversations, Pounce recreates the feeling of walking into a physical store with friends which makes online shopping more **natural, trustworthy, and engaging**.

**Agentic Commerce Hackathon**

Pounce is built as a complete **end-to-end agentic commerce platform**, where AI agents assist throughout the entire shopping journey while users remain in full control. From discovering products to completing purchases, every stage is powered by specialized agents working together securely and intelligently.

## How It Works

- **AI-Powered Shopping**  
  Specialized AI agents understand user preferences, recommend products, answer questions, and retrieve items from live merchant catalogs through **Shopify UCP**, creating a personalized shopping experience.

- **Secure Agentic Checkout**  
  During checkout, **Prava** issues a **single-use payment credential** that is restricted to one merchant and one approved amount. The AI agent completes the purchase on the user's behalf, while the actual card details never reach either Pounce or the merchant.

- **Autonomous Purchase Automation**
    With **Pounce Watcher**, users can set a persistent intent such as *"Buy these when my size is back in stock."* Setting this up requires a **one-time passkey authorization**, where the user approves a **Prava mandate** that is scoped to a specific merchant and capped at a maximum amount.
Once authorized, the watcher continuously monitors product availability and pricing in the background. When the specified condition is met, it automatically charges the **pre-authorized Prava mandate** without requiring another passkey. Prava then issues a **single-use payment credential** restricted to the approved merchant and amount, allowing the AI agent to securely complete the purchase on the user's behalf while the user's card details never reach either Pounce or the merchant.

## Why It Matters

Pounce demonstrates what **agentic commerce** is meant to be: AI agents handle product discovery, recommendations, monitoring, and checkout autonomously, while **passkey-based approval and Prava's secure payment infrastructure** ensure users always stay in complete control of their purchases.

### Nextrace(CrossLink)
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/nextracecrosslink-bf35) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/chaudhuriahana2007-art/nextrace_crosslink) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://nextrace-crosslink.vercel.app/) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://youtu.be/Y8NWG0rHaDg) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-DOMINION%202026-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://dominion2026.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-6-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> Fast solution for critical moments

![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![TensorFlow](https://img.shields.io/badge/TensorFlow-333333?style=flat-square) ![TypeScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/TypeScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Tailwind CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tailwind%20CSS-333333?style=flat-square) ![Supabase](https://img.shields.io/badge/Supabase-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

Crosslink solves the problem of delayed and disconnected missing-person searches. Today, information about a missing person may stay with one police station, so a sighting in another city or district may not be matched quickly. Crosslink connects these reports and sightings in one system and uses AI to identify possible matches in real time.

**Challenges we ran into**

Building CrossLink required solving several critical issues across vector mathematics, computer vision limitations, and frontend data handling.

Vector Metric Discrepancy: We initially configured pgvector and face-api.js to use Cosine similarity. We discovered that this metric ignores vector magnitude, crushing real human faces into a dangerously narrow band and causing massive false positives (22 out of 24 incorrect matches during testing). We had to drop the database index and migrate the entire matching architecture to Euclidean (L2) distance to achieve accurate cluster gaps.

Low-Resolution False Confidence: We found that small input faces lacked enough visual feature density, causing the recognition model to mathematically "guess" missing details and confidently match completely different strangers. We solved this by engineering a strict 110px dimensional guardrail that blocks low-quality crops before they hit the database.

Metadata Destruction: We originally planned to extract EXIF GPS data server-side, but realized our canvas re-encoding step (captureFrame()) permanently destroyed the tags. We had to shift EXIF extraction entirely to the client-side browser before processing. Furthermore, social platforms like WhatsApp strip metadata entirely, requiring us to build graceful fallback UI badges (no-gps, no-exif) instead of relying on guaranteed coordinates.

Atomic Deployment Synchronization: Overhauling the database structure—such as changing date inputs to timestamptz and rebuilding the HNSW vector index—required strict deployment sequencing. We had to carefully choreograph Supabase SQL migrations immediately before Vercel GitHub pushes to prevent the production app from crashing due to mismatched data types during live updates.

Edge Security Integration: Securing the admin dashboard and API routes without exposing credentials or leaving backdoors required running authentication at the network edge. We implemented Vercel Edge middleware utilizing HTTP-only cookies and HMAC-SHA256 signing to lock down the routes seamlessly.

Team **NexTrace** -- Aryan Jaiswal, AHANA CHAUDHURI, DHRITI GOYAL, Aditya Sharma

### IRIS Protocol

> Autonomous Capital. Intelligent Allocation.

![Node.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![JavaScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/JavaScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![SQL](https://img.shields.io/badge/SQL-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![Tailwind CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tailwind%20CSS-333333?style=flat-square) ![Neural Network](https://img.shields.io/badge/Neural%20Network-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

Inefficiencies in Modern Financial Systems
Current capital deployment frameworks are inherently constrained by legacy structures. Today, capital is generally:

Static: Allocations lack the agility to adapt dynamically to rapidly shifting market conditions.

Human-Limited: Decision-making relies on manual interventions, which introduce latency and cognitive bias.

Opaque: Stakeholders lack real-time transparency into underlying strategies and evolving risk exposures.

Reactive: Traditional systems respond retroactively to market movements rather than anticipating them.

Systemic Consequences
These structural limitations create measurable friction in the market, leading to:

Missed alpha generation in high-velocity, fast-moving markets.

Suboptimal capital allocation and underutilized liquidity.

Heightened vulnerability to market volatility and outlier events.

A fundamental misalignment between performance outcomes and stakeholder incentives.

The Core Gap & Market Opportunity
The Gap: There is currently no institutional framework that allows capital to autonomously analyze, adapt, and optimize itself in real time.

The Opportunity: While global markets generate unprecedented volumes of data continuously, this information remains fragmented and underutilized. Traditional infrastructure is simply not equipped to translate this data into intelligent, immediate action.

**Challenges we ran into**

1. Architecting a Real-Time Intelligent System
Developing a framework where autonomous agents continuously analyze, decide, and adapt required overcoming significant architectural complexities. Key hurdles included managing distributed state across multiple agents, optimizing for low-latency decision loops, and maintaining strict synchronization with live data feeds.

2. Implementing Continuous Learning via Active Regression
Transitioning from static training to a dynamic, on-the-fly learning model presented unique data science challenges. We had to design safeguards against system instability during frequent weight updates, mitigate the risk of overfitting to transient streaming data, and sustain computational performance during real-time model retraining.

3. Orchestrating Multi-Agent Coordination
Unlike traditional monolithic models, IRIS utilizes an ecosystem of competing AI agents. This required developing robust Multi-Weight Update (MWU) logic for equitable capital allocation, engineering conflict-resolution protocols to prevent execution collisions, and ensuring systemic stability despite internal agent competition.

4. Engineering a Low-Latency Data Pipeline
Processing high-velocity financial streams necessitated a highly robust data infrastructure. Challenges included establishing fault-tolerant ingestion channels via WebSockets and APIs, executing clean feature engineering with sub-millisecond latency, and guaranteeing absolute data consistency without lag.

5. Developing an Institutional-Grade Terminal Interface
Designing a client interface on par with industry-standard financial terminals required balancing information density with cognitive load. The challenge lay in rendering high-frequency live data and complex visualizations smoothly, ensuring a responsive, professional user experience without overwhelming the client side.

6. Cross-Stack System Integration
The protocol represents a complex convergence of diverse technologies. Harmonizing predictive AI models, a LangGraph-based agentic framework, decentralized blockchain logic, and a high-performance frontend terminal into a cohesive, seamless ecosystem was a primary technical undertaking.

7. Dynamic Model Deployment and State Persistence
Maintaining continuity required a robust pipeline to handle continuous model state serialization, facilitate seamless, zero-downtime updates, and ensure secure, real-time synchronization with our backend cloud infrastructure.

**Best Use of Gemini API**

In IRIS Protocol, Google Gemini functions as the cognitive layer that automatically assigns distinct "personalities" and strategic roles to our diverse suite of AI agents. By utilizing customized system prompts, Gemini transforms standard statistical models into specialized, autonomous decision-makers—such as momentum strategists, arbitrage optimizers, or volatility analysts. This automated persona assignment ensures that each agent operates with a unique behavioral profile, allowing them to independently reason through real-world market context, provide explainable logic, and execute highly differentiated trading strategies.

**Best Build on Solana**

In IRIS Protocol, we primarily leverage Solana's high-speed infrastructure to enforce agent accountability through a robust on-chain staking and slashing mechanism. Each autonomous AI agent is required to stake tokens, creating a trustless economic incentive model where underperformance results in immediate programmatic penalties. Beyond this core staking utility, Solana serves as our overarching execution and settlement layer, providing the ultra-low latency and near-zero fees necessary for real-time, non-custodial trade execution and capital allocation. By anchoring agent performance and financial state updates on-chain, Solana transforms IRIS from a standard predictive AI model into a fully transparent, verifiable, and economically aligned autonomous financial protocol.

**Best Build on Algorand**

Gemini said
In IRIS Protocol, we leverage Algorand as a highly secure, deterministic execution layer to facilitate our autonomous, AI-driven capital allocation. By utilizing Algorand’s near-instant transaction finality and robust security guarantees, we ensure that high-frequency decisions generated by our AI agents are executed immediately, without ambiguity or MEV-related unpredictability. We employ Algorand Standard Assets (ASA) to efficiently tokenize user deposits, agent stakes, and performance rewards, enabling the lightweight, low-cost micro-transactions necessary for continuous portfolio rebalancing. Furthermore, Algorand Smart Contracts seamlessly enforce our core vault logic and agent staking mechanisms, creating a trustless environment where economic accountability and capital flow are executed with absolute precision.

**Best Build on Stellar**

In IRIS Protocol, we leverage the Stellar network as our global settlement and fiat bridging layer. By utilizing Stellar's robust anchor ecosystem, IRIS seamlessly connects traditional financial rails with on-chain digital assets (like USDC), effectively eliminating onboarding friction and enabling borderless capital accessibility. Furthermore, Stellar’s near-zero transaction costs and instant settlement capabilities allow our autonomous AI agents to efficiently execute high-frequency micro-transactions, dynamically rebalance portfolios, and distribute real-world payouts in real time. Ultimately, Stellar bridges the gap between traditional finance and decentralized execution, transforming IRIS into a globally accessible and financially inclusive intelligence network.

### Vitality
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/vitality-6dae) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/Angel05Mendparaa/vitality) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://vitality-azure.vercel.app/) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-AceHack%205.0-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://acehack5.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-6-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> Where AI Meets New Life.

![React](https://img.shields.io/badge/React-333333?style=flat-square) ![CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/CSS-333333?style=flat-square) ![JavaScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/JavaScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Machine Learning](https://img.shields.io/badge/Machine%20Learning-333333?style=flat-square) ![Artificial Intelligence](https://img.shields.io/badge/Artificial%20Intelligence-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![Tailwind CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tailwind%20CSS-333333?style=flat-square) ![RestAPIs](https://img.shields.io/badge/RestAPIs-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

**The Problem**
In IVF, selecting the right embryo to transfer is the most critical decision in the entire procedure. Today, it's made by a single embryologist visually inspecting microscope images with no standardized scoring system, no second opinion, and no consistency across clinics or shifts. Fatigue, experience level, and subjectivity all affect the outcome, and a wrong selection costs the patient ₹3–5 lakh, 12 months, and another emotionally devastating cycle.

**What We Built**
Vitality brings AI-assisted embryo selection to any clinic, anywhere  using just a standard microscope photo. No new equipment. No massive upfront cost. Upload an image, get an objective viability score, a ranked list of all embryos, and a plain-English clinical explanation  all in under 2 seconds.

**How It Works**
An embryologist takes a microscope photo of each embryo and uploads it to Vitality . The AI analyses each image, scores it, ranks all embryos from best to worst, and generates a plain-English clinical explanation for each result — all in under 2 seconds, on any device.

**Challenges we ran into**

***Challenges:***

**Dataset Scarcity**  Embryo microscopic imaging data is extremely rare and sensitive. Sourcing a viable dataset was one of the hardest parts of the project  we eventually found the ICIPLAB dataset on Kaggle with 840 images.

**Single Image Constraint:** Unlike competitors who rely on 5 days of time-lapse video, vitality had to deliver accurate results from just one still image  a significantly harder problem that we solved without requiring any additional hardware.

**Model Architecture Decisions:** Choosing the right backbone was non-trivial. We needed something powerful enough to extract fine-grained embryo morphology from microscopic images, but lightweight enough to run on a free-tier cloud server. Settled on EfficientNetB3 after balancing accuracy vs inference speed.

**Best Use of Gemini API**

**Gemini API:**  Clinical AI Insights
Instead of showing raw numbers to embryologists, Gemini transforms the model's output viability score, fragmentation ratio, symmetry score, cell count — into a plain-English clinical explanation specific to each embryo. This bridges the gap between AI output and real clinical decision-making, making the tool usable by embryologists of all experience levels without needing to interpret raw probabilities.

**Best Use of Auth0**

**Auth0:**  Authentication & Clinic Access Control
Medical data is sensitive. Auth0 ensured that only verified, authenticated embryologists and clinic staff could access the platform  not just anyone with the link. It handled login, session management, and secure access without us having to build authentication from scratch, letting us focus on the core AI product.

Team **Layer3** -- [Angel Mendpara](https://github.com/Angel05Mendparaa), [Jinal Bhamwala](https://github.com/Jinalbhamwala), [yash Dave](https://github.com/yashdave182)

> Security Gateway for AI Applications

![HTML](https://img.shields.io/badge/HTML-333333?style=flat-square) ![CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/CSS-333333?style=flat-square) ![JavaScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/JavaScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![TensorFlow](https://img.shields.io/badge/TensorFlow-333333?style=flat-square) ![OpenCV](https://img.shields.io/badge/OpenCV-333333?style=flat-square) ![SQLite](https://img.shields.io/badge/SQLite-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

## The Problem It Solves

AI assistants and agents accept natural language input, which makes them vulnerable to prompt injection attacks. Attackers can manipulate prompts to override system instructions, extract sensitive data, or trigger unintended tool actions.

Wren acts as a **security gateway between applications and AI models**. Every request is inspected before reaching the model, allowing Wren to detect malicious prompts, enforce policies, and block unsafe actions.

This enables developers to safely deploy AI features without building complex security layers themselves.

### What People Can Use It For

- Protect AI chatbots and copilots from prompt injection  
- Secure AI agents that can call tools or APIs  
- Monitor AI security events through a dashboard  
- Enforce custom policies for AI interactions

**Challenges we ran into**

One challenge was **linking the gateway, authentication backend, and dashboard** so API keys, logs, and blocked prompts could sync in real time. Since the gateway intercepts every AI request, it had to validate keys and log events correctly, which required debugging multiple request flows.

Another challenge was **detecting prompt injection patterns**. Attacks can be phrased in many ways, so we built a large suspicious phrase rule set and trained a **fine-tuned DistilBERT model** to detect semantic similarities and assign a suspicion score.

Team **Carrick_Ball** -- [John Samuel](https://github.com/JoSParker), [Kushagra Shukla](https://github.com/kushu30), [Prajakta Naik](https://github.com/PrajaktaNaik17)

### Unsaid
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/unsaid-0185) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://drive.google.com/file/d/1SkqV2WxNoeAlYdu-OknO5xC7Dc1WRm2z/view?usp=drive_link) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://youtube.com/shorts/e5iKFEnENVc?feature=share) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-DOMINION%202026-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://dominion2026.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-5-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> See the patterns. Understand the words.

![Android SDK](https://img.shields.io/badge/Android%20SDK-333333?style=flat-square) ![SQLite](https://img.shields.io/badge/SQLite-333333?style=flat-square) ![Kotlin](https://img.shields.io/badge/Kotlin-333333?style=flat-square) ![NLP](https://img.shields.io/badge/NLP-333333?style=flat-square) ![Mediapipe](https://img.shields.io/badge/Mediapipe-333333?style=flat-square)

**Challenges we ran into**

**Running Models Locally on Mobile Hardware:** Standard language models require massive cloud servers to operate. Getting natural language processing to run directly on an Android device without draining memory, causing UI lag, or demanding active internet connectivity required extensive optimization and lightweight architecture.

**Parsing Mixed Regional Dialects (Romanized version of Hindi and Telugu):** Standard NLP models are primarily trained on formal English and miss colloquial, code-mixed nuances. People regularly blend languages in everyday texts, so building pattern-detection rules that accurately interpret informal phrasing across English, Hindi, and Telugu without generating false flags was a major technical hurdle.

**Ensuring Explainability and Objective Feedback:** We had to make sure the app never acted as an arbitrary "black box" or assigned clinical psychological labels to people. Structuring the engine to extract and visually highlight exact textual evidence while providing calm, de-escalating response suggestions required careful prompt and UI design.

**Time-Constrained Build and Gradle Synchronization:** Building native Android UI across multiple tabs, configuring speech input, and integrating local inference within a strict 2-hour hackathon window meant resolving Gradle dependency conflicts and catalog errors rapidly under tight time pressure.

**The problem it solves**

**Hidden Toxic Dynamics:** Unhealthy communication patterns such as gaslighting, blame-shifting, and dismissive language frequently hide inside everyday, casual conversations. Because these phrases sound ordinary, they are difficult to recognize in the moment, cause intense self-doubt, and easily become normalized over time.

**The Privacy Barrier:** People want clarity and a neutral perspective on confusing messages, but personal chats are deeply sensitive. Users cannot, and should not, upload private conversations to cloud-based AI systems where personal data could be harvested, logged, or exposed.

**On-Device Clarity Without Compromise:** UNSAID solves this dilemma by keeping all language analysis 100% on the user's phone. It functions entirely offline, detecting manipulative dynamics across English and regional dialects like romanized versions of Hindi and Telugu while highlighting the exact words that triggered the alert. Users get objective clarity and psychological safety with zero cloud exposure and complete data privacy.

Team **TwoFold** -- S.Nikhitha Reddy, S.Nishitha Reddy

### Netra
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/netra-39eb) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/iamthesharath/Netra) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-DOMINION%202026-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://dominion2026.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-5-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> Turn 60 hours of CCTV footage into a 5-minute miss

![HTML](https://img.shields.io/badge/HTML-333333?style=flat-square) ![React](https://img.shields.io/badge/React-333333?style=flat-square) ![Node.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![JavaScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/JavaScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![OpenCV](https://img.shields.io/badge/OpenCV-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![Flutter](https://img.shields.io/badge/Flutter-333333?style=flat-square) ![FFmpeg](https://img.shields.io/badge/FFmpeg-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

When a person goes missing, police must manually review hundreds of hours of CCTV footage — a process that takes days and often comes too late. Netra automates this by scanning footage using AI face recognition, instantly surfacing every moment the missing person appears across all cameras, with timestamps, confidence scores, and video clips — turning days of manual review into minutes.

**Challenges we ran into**

Engineering Challenges

CCTV Accuracy: Real-world footage is often blurry, low-light, side-angled, or partially obstructed. Extensive testing was needed to tune ArcFace similarity thresholds and balance false positives and negatives.
Performance: CPU-based InsightFace processing was extremely slow. Integrating CUDA with ONNX Runtime GPU reduced video processing time from nearly an hour to just minutes.
Android Compatibility: Android 16’s HEIC images caused Flutter preview crashes. We solved this by automatically converting selected images to JPEGbefore processing.
Privacy & Security: Netra was designed with privacy-first principles—non-matching faces are not stored, officer actions are audit-logged, and safeguards are built in to prevent misuse and mass surveillance.

> Don't just detect phishing—defeat it.

![React](https://img.shields.io/badge/React-333333?style=flat-square) ![Node.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![Machine Learning](https://img.shields.io/badge/Machine%20Learning-333333?style=flat-square) ![Express.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Express.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![TypeScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/TypeScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Tailwind CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tailwind%20CSS-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

•Traditional security systems rely on static rules and often fail to detect modern, context-aware cyber attacks. 

•Phishing attacks are rapidly increasing, with a 300%+ rise in AI-generated phishing campaigns.

•A major “human gap” exists, as more than 60% of users cannot correctly identify phishing emails.

•Hidden links, shortened URLs, and redirection chains make threat detection difficult for average users.

•The lack of real-time threat analysis increases vulnerability to scams and cyber fraud.

•These security failures contribute to over $10B in annual global financial losses from cyber fraud.

**Challenges we ran into**

•Handling asynchronous Groq API requests initially caused UI unresponsiveness and latency issues.

•We implemented strict TypeScript state management and UI loaders to prevent duplicate user submissions during analysis.

•Standard browser extensions were interfering with our network requests during local testing and debugging.

• We overrode our default IDE settings to launch the Brave browser instead, ensuring a clean, isolated environment for accurate testing.

• Passing raw, unsanitized user URLs directly into the AI prompt occasionally caused formatting errors.

• Complex URL query parameters and encoded characters were triggering false positive phishing verdicts from the model.

• We developed an Express.js middleware utility to parse and strip unnecessary tracking queries before sending the URL to the Groq API.

### Lethus
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/lethus-8b2d) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/0xteamCookie/Lethus) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://lethus.getmyroom.in/chat) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://www.youtube.com/watch?v=ph8Vugawndc) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-HackSRM%207.0-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://hack-srm26.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-5-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> Stop paying your LLM to re-read the conversation.

![Node.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Node.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![PostgreSQL](https://img.shields.io/badge/PostgreSQL-333333?style=flat-square) ![Express.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Express.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![TypeScript](https://img.shields.io/badge/TypeScript-333333?style=flat-square) ![Tailwind CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tailwind%20CSS-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

If you build LLM apps, you know the context window problem. As conversations get longer, you have to send the entire chat history back to the API on every single request just so the AI doesn't lose the plot. This burns through tokens, spikes your API bill, and actually degrades the AI's reasoning because the prompt gets bloated with irrelevant history.
Lethus solves this. It’s a drop-in proxy that sits between your client and LLM. Instead of blindly sending everything, it acts as an intelligent memory manager that dynamically cuts out the noise. Saving around 33% average cost. Example: 800M tokens/month, 80/20 input-output split, $5/M input pricing, Claude Opus 4.6, saving around $12k yearly.

**Challenges we ran into**

The "Time-Traveling" Context Bug: We used a modified version of Kadane’s algorithm to group highly relevant historical turns into continuous blocks of memory. But initially, if the vector search found high scores for Turn 3 and Turn 8, the algorithm stitched them together, completely ignoring the massive 4-turn gap in the middle. It resulted in incoherent prompts.

Lethus uses a "supersession" feature in its background Changelog to kill off old decisions when a user changes their mind (e.g., switching from PostgreSQL to SQLite). Originally, I used simple keyword overlap to detect these contradictions. But common words like "using" or "database" were triggering false positives, causing the AI to prematurely "forget" valid project constraints.

**Grand Prize**

Lethus tackles one of the most expensive and universal pain points in modern software development: LLM context bloat. Every company building AI apps is burning money and degrading model reasoning by blindly sending massive, bloated chat histories back and forth.

Lethus is a Grand Prize contender because it offers a complete, production-ready solution to this massive problem with zero integration friction. It’s not just a cool concept; it’s a fully functional, drop-in proxy. By simply changing a single URL string in the OpenAI SDK, developers get instant ROI-slashing token costs by up to 33% in average, eliminating hallucination from context fatigue, and gaining infinite AI memory.

Team **teamCookie()** -- [Kathrina Elangbam](https://github.com/Kathrina-dev), [Pranjal Kumar](https://github.com/pranjal-kumar-0), Shaunak Choudhury, [Rakesh Kumar Rabha](https://github.com/0xPixelNinja)

### Senseway
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/senseway-5fd6) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/HimalGarg/SensceWay.git) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://www.canva.com/design/DAHAq0PXoFU/F24S9_H9cjcDOQCqc4h2rg/edit?utm_content=DAHAq0PXoFU&utm_campaign=designshare&utm_medium=link2&utm_source=sharebutton) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://youtu.be/cxe0SZki1xk) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-HackTU%207.0-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://hacktu7.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-5-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> Preserving intent when motion fails !

![HTML](https://img.shields.io/badge/HTML-333333?style=flat-square) ![OpenCV](https://img.shields.io/badge/OpenCV-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![Tailwind CSS](https://img.shields.io/badge/Tailwind%20CSS-333333?style=flat-square) ![Mediapipe](https://img.shields.io/badge/Mediapipe-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

Digital systems today are designed around precise and sustained motor control. Tasks such as clicking a button, typing a message, or keeping a cursor steady assume that users can reliably execute fine hand movements.

For people with motor disabilities—including those affected by ALS, Parkinson’s disease, stroke, spinal injuries, Carpal Tunnel Syndrome (CTS), and repetitive strain injuries (RSI)—this assumption does not hold. In these conditions, cognitive intent remains intact, but the ability to translate that intent into accurate physical actions gradually degrades due to tremors, muscle weakness, fatigue, or loss of motor coordination.

As motor control declines, traditional input methods fail first. Even simple interactions become exhausting, error-prone, or impossible, leading to loss of independence, reduced access to digital services, and increased reliance on others. Existing assistive tools often rely on expensive hardware, fixed configurations, or rigid interaction models that do not adapt to real-world variability or progressive motor decline.

The core problem is not a lack of intelligence or decision-making ability—it is the absence of interaction systems that can tolerate noisy, unstable, and fatigued motor signals while still preserving user intent.

Senseway addresses this fundamental gap by focusing on the failure point of modern interfaces: their inability to support users when movement becomes unreliable, but intent remains clear.

**Challenges we ran into**

A major challenge was achieving stable and accurate eye tracking using standard webcams, as variations in lighting, camera quality, and head movement introduced significant noise in eye landmark detection. Distinguishing intentional blinks from natural blinks was also difficult, requiring careful tuning of EAR thresholds, frame counters, and cooldown logic to reduce false positives without increasing user fatigue.

Another challenge was the lack of real-world, disability-specific datasets for eye-gaze behavior, which forced us to rely on manual calibration and iterative testing rather than large-scale training data. Integrating eye gaze, blink detection, and voice input into a single real-time pipeline introduced synchronization and performance challenges. Finally, executing system-level actions safely required additional confirmation logic to prevent accidental inputs, especially for critical actions.

**Google Gemini**

In Senseway, the Gemini API is integrated as a natural language intent classification layer for emergency-related interactions. Voice input from the user is first converted to text using a speech-to-text pipeline. The resulting transcript is then passed to the Gemini API for semantic analysis.

Gemini is used to classify and validate emergency intent rather than execute actions directly. The API processes unstructured natural language input and returns a structured interpretation indicating whether the intent corresponds to an emergency scenario (e.g., medical help, contacting emergency services, or notifying a predefined emergency contact).

Once an emergency intent is confirmed by Gemini, the system extracts relevant parameters such as the emergency type and target service. This structured output is passed to a controlled execution layer, where system-level API calls (messaging, dialing emergency numbers, or triggering alerts) are initiated only after an explicit confirmation step via gaze or voice.

This design decouples intent understanding from action execution, reducing false positives and preventing accidental emergency triggers. Gemini is used strictly for semantic intent validation, while all critical actions are handled by deterministic, rule-based system logic.

Team **Senseway** -- [Bhumi Garg](https://github.com/bhumigarg0904), [Khushi Gupta](https://github.com/khushi19-code), [Aditya Kumar](https://github.com/adityafilesx), [Anas Jamshed](https://github.com/anasjamshed02-alt), [Himal Garg](https://github.com/HimalGarg)

### VisionOps
[![Devfolio](https://img.shields.io/badge/Devfolio-View%20Project-4B32C3?style=flat-square&logo=devfolio&logoColor=white)](https://devfolio.co/projects/visionops-ff42) [![GitHub](https://img.shields.io/badge/GitHub-Source%20Code-181717?style=flat-square&logo=github&logoColor=white)](https://github.com/ebrahimgamdiwala/DEVfans_hn4) [![Demo](https://img.shields.io/badge/Demo-Live-00C853?style=flat-square&logo=googlechrome&logoColor=white)](https://drive.google.com/drive/folders/1REs1g0CqCsMUQN10PwUWetLGURb0Gi3I) [![Video](https://img.shields.io/badge/Video-Watch-FF0000?style=flat-square&logo=youtube&logoColor=white)](https://youtu.be/jO0AuhpiNOE) [![Built at](https://img.shields.io/badge/Built%20at-HackNiche%204.0-0052CC?style=flat-square)](https://hackniche4-0.devfolio.co) ![Likes](https://img.shields.io/badge/Likes-4-FF6B6B?style=flat-square)

> AI That Protects Before Accidents Happen.

![Next.js](https://img.shields.io/badge/Next.js-333333?style=flat-square) ![Python](https://img.shields.io/badge/Python-333333?style=flat-square) ![PostgreSQL](https://img.shields.io/badge/PostgreSQL-333333?style=flat-square) ![Computer Vision](https://img.shields.io/badge/Computer%20Vision-333333?style=flat-square)

**The problem it solves**

Industrial environments like construction sites and factories face high safety risks, where workers are required to wear Personal Protective Equipment (PPE) such as helmets and safety vests. However, PPE compliance is typically monitored manually through supervisors or CCTV footage, which is inconsistent, labor-intensive, and often reactive.

As a result:

-Safety violations go unnoticed in real time
-Accidents occur due to delayed intervention
-There is no scalable way to monitor large workforces continuously

Our system addresses this by:

Continuously monitoring workers using AI-based computer vision
Detecting whether required PPE is being worn in real time
Instantly flagging violations and enabling proactive intervention
Reducing dependency on manual supervision
Improving overall workplace safety and compliance

**Challenges we ran into**

The biggest hurdle was a class-mapping mismatch between training and app integration.
My dataset classes were correct in Roboflow, but different parts of the pipeline were expecting slightly different labels (for example no_gloves vs no-gloves) and sometimes relying on hardcoded class order. That caused wrong labels in predictions even when the model itself was performing well.

I also hit path issues because the dataset folder name had spaces, which broke some hardcoded training scripts.

How I solved it
I made the training flow read class names directly from data.yaml every time.
I removed hardcoded assumptions about class order and used the model’s exported names mapping.
I generated a class map artifact for integration so backend and frontend both consume the same source of truth.
I replaced brittle fixed paths with auto-detection of dataset root and YAML location.
I added quick validation/inference checks right after training to catch label mismatches immediately.
Outcome
After these fixes, the training pipeline became reproducible and integration-safe:
the same class IDs and names are now consistent across notebook training, backend inference, and frontend display.
